4	The translations are not always word-for-word translations, but they strive for natural expressions in each language. A machine translator has been used in them as an aid. Vosmuosi ej oo kina ut sa tu sa vosmuosi, mut in itsi oota tutraaen mu biocuova kautsasi in obki vos. Vosmuuvö oota teooen in itsi na vyo.
